User clicks \\u0026quot;Continue with Google\\u0026quot; on login page         │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  2. Client calls GET /api/auth/google                        │\\n│     └─ Server constructs Google OAuth URL with:              │\\n│        ├─ client_id: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID                        │\\n│        ├─ redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI                  │\\n│        ├─ scope: openid email profile                        │\\n│        └─ response_type: code                                │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  3. Browser redirects to Google's consent screen            │\\n│     └─ User selects Google account and grants permission     │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  4. Google redirects back to:                                │\\n│     GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI?code={auth_code}                     │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  5. GET /api/auth/google/callback?code={auth_code}          │\\n│     ├─ Exchange auth_code for tokens (Google API)            │\\n│     │   POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token             │\\n│     │   Body: code, client_id, client_secret,                │\\n│     │         redirect_uri, grant_type=authorization_code    │\\n│     ├─ Decode id_token to get user profile                   │\\n│     │   ├─ Google user ID                                    │\\n│     │   ├─ Email                                             │\\n│     │   └─ Name / Avatar                                     │\\n│     └─ Find or create user in database                       │\\n│         ├─ If user exists: update Google ID, login           │\\n│         └─ If new user: create account with FREE tier,       │\\n│            ₹1,00,000 virtual capital                         │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  6. Generate JWT token for session                           │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  7. Redirect to /dashboard?token={jwt}                       │\\n│     └─ Client stores token from URL params                   │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                          │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  9. User is logged in and on dashboard                       │\\n│                                                              │\\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\\\"\\u003e\\u003cpre class=\\\"notranslate\\\"\\u003e\\u003ccode\\u003e┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\\n│                   GOOGLE OAUTH FLOW                         │\\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\\n│                                                              │\\n│  1. User clicks \\\"Continue with Google\\\" on login page         │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  2. Client calls GET /api/auth/google                        │\\n│     └─ Server constructs Google OAuth URL with:              │\\n│        ├─ client_id: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID                        │\\n│        ├─ redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI                  │\\n│        ├─ scope: openid email profile                        │\\n│        └─ response_type: code                                │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  3. Browser redirects to Google's consent screen            │\\n│     └─ User selects Google account and grants permission     │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  4. Google redirects back to:                                │\\n│     GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI?code={auth_code}                     │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  5. GET /api/auth/google/callback?code={auth_code}          │\\n│     ├─ Exchange auth_code for tokens (Google API)            │\\n│     │   POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token             │\\n│     │   Body: code, client_id, client_secret,                │\\n│     │         redirect_uri, grant_type=authorization_code    │\\n│     ├─ Decode id_token to get user profile                   │\\n│     │   ├─ Google user ID                                    │\\n│     │   ├─ Email                                             │\\n│     │   └─ Name / Avatar                                     │\\n│     └─ Find or create user in database                       │\\n│         ├─ If user exists: update Google ID, login           │\\n│         └─ If new user: create account with FREE tier,       │\\n│            ₹1,00,000 virtual capital                         │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  6. Generate JWT token for session                           │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  7. Redirect to /dashboard?token={jwt}                       │\\n│     └─ Client stores token from URL params                   │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                          │\\n│                        │                                     │\\n│  9. User navigates to /login                             │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  2. Clicks \\\"Sign Up\\\" tab                                │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  3. Enters Email + Password                             │\",\"│     └─ Password validation: min 8 chars, strength check │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  4. Clicks \\\"Register\\\"                                   │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  5. Backend validates input                             │\",\"│     ├─ Check email format                               │\",\"│     ├─ Check password strength                          │\",\"│     └─ Check email uniqueness in database               │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  6. Create user record in PostgreSQL                    │\",\"│     ├─ Hash password (bcrypt)                           │\",\"│     ├─ Assign FREE tier                                 │\",\"│     ├─ Set virtual capital: ₹1,00,000                   │\",\"│     └─ Create portfolio record                          │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  7. Login Flow\",\"\",\"Returning users authenticate with credentials or Google OAuth.\",\"\",\"```\",\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\",\"│                      LOGIN FLOW                         │\",\"├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\",\"│                                                          │\",\"│  1. User navigates to /login                             │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  2. Selects login method:                               │\",\"│     ├─ Email + Password                                 │\",\"│     └─ Google OAuth (see Section 3)                     │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  3. Enters Email + Password                             │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  4. POST /api/auth/login                                │\",\"│     ├─ Find user by email                               │\",\"│     ├─ Compare password hash (bcrypt)                   │\",\"│     └─ Verify account is active                         │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  5. Generate JWT token                                  │\",\"│     ├─ Payload: { userId, email, role, tier }           │\",\"│     ├─ Signed with JWT_SECRET                           │\",\"│     └─ Expiry: JWT_EXPIRES_IN (default: 7d)            │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  6. Return JWT + user profile to client                 │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  7. Client stores JWT in localStorage                   │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                     │\",\"│     └─ Connect to NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL                    │\",\"│                    │                                     │\",\"│  9. Redirect to /dashboard                              │\",\"│                                                          │\",\"└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\",\"```\",\"\",\"### Login Error Cases\",\"\",\"| Scenario | Behavior |\",\"|----------|----------|\",\"| User not found | Return 404 — \\\"No account found with this email\\\" |\",\"| Wrong password | Return 401 — \\\"Invalid credentials\\\" |\",\"| Account disabled | Return 403 — \\\"Account has been disabled\\\" |\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## 3. Google OAuth Flow\",\"\",\"Users can authenticate using their Google account.\",\"\",\"```\",\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\",\"│                   GOOGLE OAUTH FLOW                         │\",\"├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\",\"│                                                              │\",\"│  1. User clicks \\\"Continue with Google\\\" on login page         │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  2. Client calls GET /api/auth/google                        │\",\"│     └─ Server constructs Google OAuth URL with:              │\",\"│        ├─ client_id: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID                        │\",\"│        ├─ redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI                  │\",\"│        ├─ scope: openid email profile                        │\",\"│        └─ response_type: code                                │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  3. Browser redirects to Google's consent screen            │\",\"│     └─ User selects Google account and grants permission     │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  4. Google redirects back to:                                │\",\"│     GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI?code={auth_code}                     │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  5. GET /api/auth/google/callback?code={auth_code}          │\",\"│     ├─ Exchange auth_code for tokens (Google API)            │\",\"│     │   POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token             │\",\"│     │   Body: code, client_id, client_secret,                │\",\"│     │         redirect_uri, grant_type=authorization_code    │\",\"│     ├─ Decode id_token to get user profile                   │\",\"│     │   ├─ Google user ID                                    │\",\"│     │   ├─ Email                                             │\",\"│     │   └─ Name / Avatar                                     │\",\"│     └─ Find or create user in database                       │\",\"│         ├─ If user exists: update Google ID, login           │\",\"│         └─ If new user: create account with FREE tier,       │\",\"│            ₹1,00,000 virtual capital                         │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  6. Generate JWT token for session                           │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  7. Redirect to /dashboard?token={jwt}                       │\",\"│     └─ Client stores token from URL params                   │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                          │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  9. User is logged in and on dashboard                       │\",\"│                                                              │\",\"└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\",\"```\",\"\",\"### Google OAuth Error Cases\",\"\",\"| Scenario | Behavior |\",\"|----------|----------|\",\"| User denies permission | Google redirects with `error=access_denied` — show login page |\",\"| Invalid auth code | Return 400 — \\\"Invalid authorization code\\\" |\",\"| Token exchange fails | Return 502 — \\\"Failed to authenticate with Google\\\" |\",\"| `GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI` mismatch | Google shows error — URIs must match exactly |\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## 4. Trade Placement Flow\",\"\",\"Users place orders to buy or sell instruments.\",\"\",\"```\",\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\",\"│                  TRADE PLACEMENT FLOW                        │\",\"├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\",\"│                                                              │\",\"│  1. User browses stocks/futures/options on respective pages │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  2. Clicks on a stock/derivative to open detail view        │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  3. Trade panel opens (BUY/SELL toggle)                      │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  4. User configures order:                                   │\",\"│     ├─ Side: BUY or SELL                                    │\",\"│     ├─ Order Type: MARKET / LIMIT / SL                      │\",\"│     ├─ Quantity: Number of shares/lots                      │\",\"│     ├─ Price: (for LIMIT/SL orders)                         │\",\"│     ├─ Stop Loss: Trigger price for auto-exit              │\",\"│     └─ Target: Profit target for auto-exit                 │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  5. Client-side validation:                                  │\",\"│     ├─ Quantity \\u003e 0                                          │\",\"│     ├─ Sufficient virtual balance/margin                    │\",\"│     ├─ Price \\u003e 0 (for LIMIT orders)                         │\",\"│     ├─ SL \\u003c current price (for BUY) or SL \\u003e current (SELL) │\",\"│     ├─ Target \\u003e current price (for BUY) or Target \\u003c (SELL) │\",\"│     └─ Premium tier check (futures/options require PREMIUM)│\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  6. POST /api/trade/order                                    │\",\"│     ├─ Verify JWT authentication                            │\",\"│     ├─ Re-validate all inputs server-side                   │\",\"│     ├─ Check market hours (if ENFORCE_MARKET_HOURS=true)   │\",\"│     ├─ Calculate brokerage (BROKERAGE_PERCENT)              │\",\"│     ├─ Check margin requirements (futures)                  │\",\"│     ├─ Create Order record in database                      │\",\"│     │   ├─ status: OPEN / FILLED / PENDING                  │\",\"│     │   ├─ type: MARKET / LIMIT / SL                        │\",\"│     │   └─ segment: EQUITY / FUTURES / OPTIONS              │\",\"│     └─ Create/Update Position record                        │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  7. For MARKET orders:                                      │\",\"│     ├─ Fill immediately at current market price             │\",\"│     └─ Create Trade record (execution log)                  │\",\"│                                                              │\",\"│  8. For LIMIT/SL orders:                                    │\",\"│     └─ Status remains PENDING until price condition met     │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  9. Return order confirmation to client                      │\",\"│     ├─ Order ID                                             │\",\"│     ├─ Fill price                                           │\",\"│     ├─ Brokerage charged                                    │\",\"│     └─ Updated available balance                            │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  10. UI updates:                                             │\",\"│      ├─ Order appears in Orders page                        │\",\"│      ├─ Position opens/appears in Positions page            │\",\"│      ├─ Portfolio value updates                             │\",\"│      └─ If SL/Target set: GlobalSLMonitor begins tracking   │\",\"│                                                              │\",\"└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\",\"```\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## 5. Auto-Exit Flow\",\"\",\"The `GlobalSLMonitor` component watches open positions and auto-squares them when SL or Target prices are hit.\",\"\",\"```\",\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\",\"│                    AUTO-EXIT FLOW                            │\",\"├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\",\"│                                                              │\",\"│  1. User has open positions with SL and/or Target set       │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  2. GlobalSLMonitor component initializes on app load       │\",\"│     └─ Fetches all open positions with SL/Target from API  │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  3. WebSocket connection streams real-time prices           │\",\"│     ├─ Yahoo Finance polling (fallback)                     │\",\"│     └─ Upstox API WebSocket (primary, real-time)            │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  4. For each price update:                                  │\",\"│     ├─ Match price to symbol in monitored positions         │\",\"│     └─ For each matching position:                          │\",\"│         ├─ If BUY position:                                 │\",\"│         │   ├─ Check: current_price \\u003c= stop_loss → SL HIT   │\",\"│         │   └─ Check: current_price \\u003e= target → TARGET HIT  │\",\"│         └─ If SELL position:                                │\",\"│             ├─ Check: current_price \\u003e= stop_loss → SL HIT   │\",\"│             └─ Check: current_price \\u003c= target → TARGET HIT  │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  5. When SL or Target is hit:                               │\",\"│     ├─ POST /api/trade/square-off                           │\",\"│     │   ├─ Position ID                                      │\",\"│     │   ├─ Current market price (trigger price)             │\",\"│     │   └─ Reason: \\\"STOP_LOSS\\\" or \\\"TARGET\\\"                 │\",\"│     ├─ Server closes position                               │\",\"│     │   ├─ Calculate final P\\u0026L                              │\",\"│     │   ├─ Deduct brokerage                                 │\",\"│     │   ├─ Update portfolio balance                         │\",\"│     │   └─ Create Trade record (exit execution)            │\",\"│     └─ Return confirmation                                  │\",\"│                        │                                     │\",\"│  6. Client updates:                                          │\",\"│     ├─ Remove from monitored positions                      │\",\"│     ├─ Update Positions page (position closed)              │\",\"│     ├─ Update Portfolio (realized P\\u0026L)                      │\",\"│     └─ Show notification: \\\"SL hit on RELIANCE — Loss ₹XXX\\\"  │\",\"│         or \\\"Target hit on TCS — Profit ₹XXX\\\"                │\",\"│                                                              │\",\"└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\",\"```\",\"\",\"### Auto-Exit Edge Cases\",\"\",\"| Scenario | Behavior |\",\"|----------|----------|\",\"| WebSocket disconnects | Reconnect and resume monitoring; check for missed triggers on reconnect |\",\"| Market closes with open SL/Target | Monitoring pauses; resumes on next market open |\",\"| SL = Target (same price) | Both conditions trigger simultaneously — position closed |\",\"| Partial fill (not applicable — paper trading) | Always full fill in paper trading |\",\"| Multiple positions same symbol | Each position monitored independently |\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## 6. Order Types\",\"\",\"| Order Type | Description | Price Behavior |\",\"|-----------|-------------|----------------|\",\"| **MARKET** | Executes immediately at the current market price | Uses latest available price from WebSocket/REST |\",\"| **LIMIT** | Executes only when market price reaches the specified limit price | BUY: triggers when price ≤ limit; SELL: triggers when price ≥ limit |\",\"| **SL (Stop Loss)** | Placed as a pending order; triggers when price crosses the stop price | BUY-SL: triggers when price ≥ stop; SELL-SL: triggers when price ≤ stop |\",\"\",\"### Order Lifecycle\",\"\",\"```\",\"PLACED → PENDING → TRIGGERED → FILLED → EXECUTED\",\"                    ↓\",\"                 EXPIRED (if not triggered within validity)\",\"                    ↓\",\"                 CANCELLED (user manually cancels)\",\"```\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## 7. Trading Segments\",\"\",\"### 7.1 Equity (Cash Market)\",\"\",\"| Attribute | Value |\",\"|-----------|-------|\",\"| Segment Code | `EQUITY` |\",\"| Instruments | NSE-listed stocks (RELIANCE, TCS, INFY, HDFC BANK, etc.) |\",\"| Lot Size | 1 share (no lot restriction) |\",\"| Margin Required | Full order value (no leverage) |\",\"| Brokerage | BROKERAGE_PERCENT of order value (min: MIN_BROKERAGE, max: MAX_BROKERAGE) |\",\"| Tier | FREE and PREMIUM |\",\"\",\"### 7.2 Futures\",\"\",\"| Attribute | Value |\",\"|-----------|-------|\",\"| Segment Code | `FUTURES` |\",\"| Instruments | NSE futures contracts (monthly expiry) |\",\"| Lot Size | Varies by instrument (e.g., NIFTY: 25 lots, RELIANCE: 250) |\",\"| Margin Required | DEFAULT_FUTURES_MARGIN_PERCENT of contract value |\",\"| Brokerage | BROKERAGE_PERCENT of notional value |\",\"| Tier | **PREMIUM only** |\",\"\",\"### 7.3 Options (CE/PE)\",\"\",\"| Attribute | Value |\",\"|-----------|-------|\",\"| Segment Code | `OPTIONS` |\",\"| Instruments | NSE options (Calls/CE and Puts/PE, weekly/monthly expiry) |\",\"| Lot Size | Varies by instrument (e.g., NIFTY: 25, BANKNIFTY: 15) |\",\"| Premium | Current market premium price × lot size |\",\"| Margin Required | Premium paid (for buyers) |\",\"| Brokerage | BROKERAGE_PERCENT of premium value |\",\"| Tier | **PREMIUM only** |\",\"\",\"### Option Types\",\"\",\"| Type | Code | Description |\",\"|------|------|-------------|\",\"| **Call Option** | CE | Right to buy at strike price. Profit increases as underlying rises. |\",\"| **Put Option** | PE | Right to sell at strike price. Profit increases as underlying falls. |\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"## Quick Reference: Full Trade Lifecycle\",\"\",\"```\",\"Browse → Select → Configure → Validate → Submit → Fill → Position Open\",\"                                                           │\",\"                                                    SL/Target Monitoring\",\"                                                           │\",\"                                                    ┌──────┴──────┐\",\"                                                 SL Hit      Target Hit\",\"                                                    │              │\",\"                                                 Auto-Square   Auto-Square\",\"                                                    │              │\",\"                                              P\\u0026L Realized   P\\u0026L Realized\",\"                                                    │              │\",\"                                              Position Closed Position Closed\",\"                                                    └──────┬───────┘\",\"                                                           │\",\"                                                    Notification Sent\",\"                                                    Portfolio Updated\",\"```\",\"\",\"---\",\"\",\"*For architecture details, see [ARCHITECTURE.md](./ARCHITECTURE.md).*  \",\"*For testing these flows, see User navigates to /login                             │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  2. Selects login method:                               │\n│     ├─ Email + Password                                 │\n│     └─ Google OAuth (see Section 3)                     │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  3. Enters Email + Password                             │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  4. POST /api/auth/login                                │\n│     ├─ Find user by email                               │\n│     ├─ Compare password hash (bcrypt)                   │\n│     └─ Verify account is active                         │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  5. Generate JWT token                                  │\n│     ├─ Payload: { userId, email, role, tier }           │\n│     ├─ Signed with JWT_SECRET                           │\n│     └─ Expiry: JWT_EXPIRES_IN (default: 7d)            │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  6. Return JWT + user profile to client                 │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  7. Client stores JWT in localStorage                   │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                     │\n│     └─ Connect to NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL                    │\n│                    │                                     │\n│  9. Redirect to /dashboard                              │\n│                                                          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      LOGIN FLOW                         │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                          │\n│  1. Google OAuth Flow</h2><a id=\"user-content-3-google-oauth-flow\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Google OAuth Flow\" href=\"#3-google-oauth-flow\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Users can authenticate using their Google account.</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\"><code>┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                   GOOGLE OAUTH FLOW                         │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                              │\n│  1. User clicks \"Continue with Google\" on login page         │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  2. Client calls GET /api/auth/google                        │\n│     └─ Server constructs Google OAuth URL with:              │\n│        ├─ client_id: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID                        │\n│        ├─ redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI                  │\n│        ├─ scope: openid email profile                        │\n│        └─ response_type: code                                │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  3. Browser redirects to Google's consent screen            │\n│     └─ User selects Google account and grants permission     │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  4. Google redirects back to:                                │\n│     GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI?code={auth_code}                     │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  5. GET /api/auth/google/callback?code={auth_code}          │\n│     ├─ Exchange auth_code for tokens (Google API)            │\n│     │   POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token             │\n│     │   Body: code, client_id, client_secret,                │\n│     │         redirect_uri, grant_type=authorization_code    │\n│     ├─ Decode id_token to get user profile                   │\n│     │   ├─ Google user ID                                    │\n│     │   ├─ Email                                             │\n│     │   └─ Name / Avatar                                     │\n│     └─ Find or create user in database                       │\n│         ├─ If user exists: update Google ID, login           │\n│         └─ If new user: create account with FREE tier,       │\n│            ₹1,00,000 virtual capital                         │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  6. Generate JWT token for session                           │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  7. Redirect to /dashboard?token={jwt}                       │\n│     └─ Client stores token from URL params                   │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  8. Initialize WebSocket connection                          │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  9. User is logged in and on dashboard                       │\n│                                                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                   GOOGLE OAUTH FLOW                         │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                              │\n│  1. User clicks &quot;Continue with Google&quot; on login page         │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  2. Client calls GET /api/auth/google                        │\n│     └─ Server constructs Google OAuth URL with:              │\n│        ├─ client_id: GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID                        │\n│        ├─ redirect_uri: GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI                  │\n│        ├─ scope: openid email profile                        │\n│        └─ response_type: code                                │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  3. Browser redirects to Google's consent screen            │\n│     └─ User selects Google account and grants permission     │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  4. Google redirects back to:                                │\n│     GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI?code={auth_code}                     │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  5. Generate JWT token for session                           │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  7. User browses stocks/futures/options on respective pages │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  2. Clicks on a stock/derivative to open detail view        │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  3. Trade panel opens (BUY/SELL toggle)                      │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  4. User configures order:                                   │\n│     ├─ Side: BUY or SELL                                    │\n│     ├─ Order Type: MARKET / LIMIT / SL                      │\n│     ├─ Quantity: Number of shares/lots                      │\n│     ├─ Price: (for LIMIT/SL orders)                         │\n│     ├─ Stop Loss: Trigger price for auto-exit              │\n│     └─ Target: Profit target for auto-exit                 │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  5. Client-side validation:                                  │\n│     ├─ Quantity &gt; 0                                          │\n│     ├─ Sufficient virtual balance/margin                    │\n│     ├─ Price &gt; 0 (for LIMIT orders)                         │\n│     ├─ SL &lt; current price (for BUY) or SL &gt; current (SELL) │\n│     ├─ Target &gt; current price (for BUY) or Target &lt; (SELL) │\n│     └─ Premium tier check (futures/options require PREMIUM)│\n│                        │                                     │\n│  6. POST /api/trade/order                                    │\n│     ├─ Verify JWT authentication                            │\n│     ├─ Re-validate all inputs server-side                   │\n│     ├─ Check market hours (if ENFORCE_MARKET_HOURS=true)   │\n│     ├─ Calculate brokerage (BROKERAGE_PERCENT)              │\n│     ├─ Check margin requirements (futures)                  │\n│     ├─ Create Order record in database                      │\n│     │   ├─ status: OPEN / FILLED / PENDING                  │\n│     │   ├─ type: MARKET / LIMIT / SL                        │\n│     │   └─ segment: EQUITY / FUTURES / OPTIONS              │\n│     └─ Create/Update Position record                        │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  7. For MARKET orders:                                      │\n│     ├─ Fill immediately at current market price             │\n│     └─ Create Trade record (execution log)                  │\n│                                                              │\n│  8. For LIMIT/SL orders:                                    │\n│     └─ Status remains PENDING until price condition met     │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  9. Return order confirmation to client                      │\n│     ├─ Order ID                                             │\n│     ├─ Fill price                                           │\n│     ├─ Brokerage charged                                    │\n│     └─ Updated available balance                            │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  10. UI updates:                                             │\n│      ├─ Order appears in Orders page                        │\n│      ├─ Position opens/appears in Positions page            │\n│      ├─ Portfolio value updates                             │\n│      └─ If SL/Target set: GlobalSLMonitor begins tracking   │\n│                                                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                  TRADE PLACEMENT FLOW                        │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                              │\n│  1. Auto-Exit Flow\" href=\"#5-auto-exit-flow\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The <code>GlobalSLMonitor</code> component watches open positions and auto-squares them when SL or Target prices are hit.</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\"><code>┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    AUTO-EXIT FLOW                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                              │\n│  1. User has open positions with SL and/or Target set       │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  2. GlobalSLMonitor component initializes on app load       │\n│     └─ Fetches all open positions with SL/Target from API  │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  3. WebSocket connection streams real-time prices           │\n│     ├─ Yahoo Finance polling (fallback)                     │\n│     └─ Upstox API WebSocket (primary, real-time)            │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  4. For each price update:                                  │\n│     ├─ Match price to symbol in monitored positions         │\n│     └─ For each matching position:                          │\n│         ├─ If BUY position:                                 │\n│         │   ├─ Check: current_price &lt;= stop_loss → SL HIT   │\n│         │   └─ Check: current_price &gt;= target → TARGET HIT  │\n│         └─ If SELL position:                                │\n│             ├─ Check: current_price &gt;= stop_loss → SL HIT   │\n│             └─ Check: current_price &lt;= target → TARGET HIT  │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  5. When SL or Target is hit:                               │\n│     ├─ POST /api/trade/square-off                           │\n│     │   ├─ Position ID                                      │\n│     │   ├─ Current market price (trigger price)             │\n│     │   └─ Reason: \"STOP_LOSS\" or \"TARGET\"                 │\n│     ├─ Server closes position                               │\n│     │   ├─ Calculate final P&amp;L                              │\n│     │   ├─ Deduct brokerage                                 │\n│     │   ├─ Update portfolio balance                         │\n│     │   └─ Create Trade record (exit execution)            │\n│     └─ Return confirmation                                  │\n│                        │                                     │\n│  6. Client updates:                                          │\n│     ├─ Remove from monitored positions                      │\n│     ├─ Update Positions page (position closed)              │\n│     ├─ Update Portfolio (realized P&amp;L)                      │\n│     └─ Show notification: \"SL hit on RELIANCE — Loss ₹XXX\"  │\n│         or \"Target hit on TCS — Profit ₹XXX\"                │\n│                                                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n</code></pre><div class=\"zeroclipboard-container\">\n    <clipboard-copy aria-label=\"Copy code to clipboard\" class=\"ClipboardButton btn btn-invisible js-clipboard-copy m-2 p-0 d-flex flex-justify-center flex-items-center\" data-copy-feedback=\"Copied!\" data-tooltip-direction=\"w\" value=\"┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    AUTO-EXIT FLOW                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│                                                              │\n│  1.       │\n│                                                              │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\" tabindex=\"0\" role=\"button\">\n      <svg d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Scenario</th>\n<th>Behavior</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>WebSocket disconnects</td>\n<td>Reconnect and resume monitoring; check for missed triggers on reconnect</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Market closes with open SL/Target</td>\n<td>Monitoring pauses; resumes on next market open</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>SL = Target (same price)</td>\n<td>Both conditions trigger simultaneously — position closed</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Partial fill (not applicable — paper trading)</td>\n<td>Always full fill in paper trading</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Multiple
